Motivated by the viewing process of SOIs, the proposed PCH includes a local cyclopean perception module, a global predictive perception module, and a visual quality regressor. First, observers browse different spherical sceneries from viewports, and aggregate the local visual information to infer the perceptual quality of SOIs. Therefore, we extract various viewports, followed by cyclopean conversion and saliency detection to approach the perception and attention of the human brain. After the local aggregation, viewers then infer the global scene in their minds. Based on the binocular mechanism, we fuse left and right views to perform predictive coding hierarchy modelling. Finally, the visual quality regressor is exploited to obtain the ultimate quality score related to both local and global perceptual cues. Extensive experiments demonstrate that the proposed PCH achieves competitive and consistently improved performance compared with state-of-the-art quality assessment methods.

</details>


### [35] [A Large-scale Evaluation of Text-guided Models for Facial Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28802)
*Rahul Nair,Saurav Pandit,Hannah Kerner*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文首次大规模评估了六种流行的文本引导扩散模型在面部编辑任务中的表现，构建了包含169个面部编辑属性的最大数据集Face-Edit-Attributes，并对约100万张图像进行评估。结果显示大多数模型在头发和配饰编辑上表现良好，但姿态编辑困难，且普遍存在过度编辑问题，尤其在深肤色男性和年老人脸上表现明显的偏见。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然文本引导的扩散模型已被广泛应用于整体场景编辑，但尚未在面部编辑任务上进行系统全面的测试。现有方法中，GANs能够进行多样化编辑但不稳定，3DMMs虽然稳定但只能修改姿态和表情，而文本引导模型有望结合两者的优势，需要对其面部编辑能力进行全面评估。

Method: 作者构建了Face-Edit-Attributes数据集，包含169个聚焦于头发、配饰和姿态编辑的面部编辑属性，这是目前最大的面部编辑属性集合。利用CelebA和CelebSET两个流行的名人面部数据集，对六种流行文本引导扩散模型进行了顺序面部编辑任务的大规模评估，共评估约100万张图像，并同时分析了各模型的人口统计学偏见。

Result: 大多数模型在头发和配饰编辑任务中表现良好，但在姿态编辑上存在困难。所有模型都存在过度编辑的问题，例如仅要求改变发型时却改变了头发颜色。在偏见分析中发现，几乎所有模型对深肤色男性面部和老年人面部产生了更多过度编辑，表现出令人惊讶的偏见。

Conclusion: 文本引导扩散模型在面部编辑中具有潜力但仍存在局限：过度编辑问题和人口统计学偏见需要进一步解决。该研究提供了首个大规模基准和约100万张图像的公开数据资源，为后续面部编辑模型的改进提供了重要参考。

Abstract: Facial appearance editing powers popular applications like FaceApp and Photoshop. Generative Adversarial Networks (GANs) and 3D Morphable Models (3DMMs) have been widely used for facial editing. GANs can perform varied facial edits (e.g., changing hair color, hairstyle), but often produce unstable edits. 3DMMs produce stable edits, but can only alter pose and facial expression. Recently, text-guided diffusion models like Nano Banana have become popular for image editing. Text-guided models are a compelling alternative to GANs and 3DMMs since they can produce both stable and varied image edits. While text-guided models have been widely tested for whole-scene edits (e.g., ``make the woman play a guitar''), they have not been comprehensively tested for facial editing. We conducted the first large-scale evaluation ($\sim1$M images evaluated) of six popular text-guided models on a sequential facial editing task. We present Face-Edit-Attributes, the largest collection of $169$ facial editing attributes focused on hair, accessories, and pose edits. We compared model performance using two popular celebrity face datasets: CelebA and CelebSET. Our results show that most models performed hair and accessory edits well, but struggled with editing pose. All models over-edit (e.g., changing hair color when asked only to change the hairstyle). We also evaluated demographic biases in each model. Our results show surprising biases in overediting: almost all models created more overedits for dark-skinned male faces and old faces. The code and data for our results (including our repository of $\sim 1$M images) can be accessed \href{https://github.com/rahul1801/Face-Edit-Bench}{\textcolor{blue}{here}}.

</details>


### [36] [FigMirror: Ground It, Code It, Plot It](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28814)
*Xiaohan Zhao,Jiacheng Liu,Yaxin Luo,Zhiqiang Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出FigMirror，一个基于智能体框架的科学图表风格迁移系统，可将参考图表的视觉风格（如配色、排版）应用到新数据绘制中。同时引入PlotTwin-Bench基准测试集用于评估。实验表明FigMirror在参考条件风格迁移任务上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法主要关注复现参考图表本身，而更实际的需求是将新数据按参考图表的视觉风格绘制。现有像素级优化方法难以将风格迁移到新数据上，缺乏有效的风格迁移框架。

Method: 提出FigMirror智能体框架，利用现代计算机使用模型的坐标定位和编码能力。核心技术是Grounded Measurement，通过坐标定位视觉元素，并通过可执行代码测量其属性。

Result: 在PlotTwin-Bench基准上，FigMirror在参考条件风格迁移任务上持续优于现有方法。论文中的所有图表均由FigMirror生成（除对比方法产生的图表外）。

Conclusion: FigMirror证明了坐标定位和编码能力是科学图表风格迁移的关键，为将参考图表的视觉风格迁移到新数据提供了一种有效的智能体解决方案。代码和数据已开源。

Abstract: Converting scientific figures into executable code has gained increasing attention, yet existing methods primarily focus on reproducing the reference figure itself. A more practical setting is to plot new data while preserving the visual style of a reference figure (e.g., color scheme and typography). Prior approaches mimic the reference through pixel-level optimization and struggle to carry its style to new data. We show that the key to this task lies in the coordinate grounding and coding capabilities present in modern computer-use models. We propose FigMirror, an agentic framework that unlocks these capabilities through Grounded Measurement, which locates visual elements by coordinates and measures their properties through executable code. We further introduce PlotTwin-Bench, an expert-curated benchmark with fine-grained code and image-level style metrics. Experiments show that FigMirror consistently outperforms existing methods on reference-conditioned style transfer. All plots in this paper are generated by FigMirror, except those produced by other methods for comparison. Our code and data are available at: https://github.com/VILA-Lab/FigMirror.

</details>


### [37] [Text-Driven Artistic Staging: Pose, Lighting, and Camera References from Paintings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28823)
*Yunge Wen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出"文本到可编辑3D场景布局"任务，从情感描述联合生成人体姿势、主光源和相机配置。通过从2,328幅具象绘画构建11,911个文本-场景配对数据集，训练流匹配Transformer模型，支持可变人数和多种布局方案，检索R@1达32.2%，远优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成方法通常独立建模人体姿势、光照和相机位置，而艺术家在创作时是协调这些要素来传达叙事和情感的，缺乏从文本联合生成可编辑3D场景布局的方法。

Method: 从具象绘画中重建SMPL人体模型、估计低频光照、恢复相机参数，并与ArtEmis情感描述配对构建数据集；训练支持可变人数的流匹配Transformer，为每个提示生成多个场景布局方案。

Result: 在留出描述上，检索R@1达32.2%，而CLIP基的最近邻检索仅为16.6%，同时大致保持语料库级别的多样性。

Conclusion: 实验结果证明了从文本生成可编辑、情感条件化的3D场景布局参考的可行性，为艺术创作和3D内容生成提供了新途径。

Abstract: Artists coordinate human pose, illumination, and camera placement to convey narrative and emotion, but existing generative methods typically model these elements independently. We introduce text-to-editable 3D staging, a task that jointly generates human poses, a dominant light, and a camera configuration from an affective description. We construct 11,911 text--staging pairs from 2,328 figurative paintings by reconstructing SMPL bodies, estimating low-frequency illumination, recovering camera parameters, and pairing each scene with ArtEmis descriptions. We train a flow-matching transformer that supports variable numbers of figures and produces multiple staging alternatives for each prompt. On held-out descriptions, the model achieves 32.2\% retrieval R@1, compared with 16.6\% for CLIP-based nearest-neighbor retrieval, while approximately preserving corpus-level diversity. These results demonstrate the feasibility of generating editable, emotionally conditioned 3D staging references from text.

</details>


### [38] [BlobBoards: Robust Markers for Accurate Pose](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28830)
*James Pritts,Till Sittart,Hendrik Sauer,Silja Janßen,Felix Seegräber,David Nakath,Kevin Köser*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出BlobBoards，一种基于高斯斑点场的基准标记系统，通过密集多尺度特征实现标记检测、识别和位姿估计，在检测率、位姿精度和遮挡鲁棒性上均达到最先进水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基准标记系统（如AprilTag和ArUco）在遮挡、小尺寸标记、大视距变化等场景下存在检测率和位姿精度不足的问题，亟需一种更鲁棒的标记方案。

Method: 设计密集多尺度高斯斑点场作为标记板，每块板注册数百个斑点特征，通过多尺度保持不同焦距/距离/倾斜下的可检测性；利用学习到的局部描述子匹配参考图案并做空间验证，对应关系同时确定位姿并验证身份。

Result: 以运动捕捉为真值，BlobBoards中位平移误差3.6-5.0mm，较AprilTag在小板降低89%、大板降低70%误差；大旋转失败远少于现有系统。检测率达80%（AprilTag 74%、ArUco 58%），最小标记上优势最大；50%遮挡下仍检测69%且中位平移误差基本不变，而AprilTag和ArUco完全失效。

Conclusion: BlobBoards在检测率、位姿精度和遮挡鲁棒性三方面均达到最先进水平，为复杂真实场景下的视觉标记定位提供了更可靠的方案。

Abstract: We propose BlobBoards, a fiducial marker system comprising a dense, multi-scale field of Gaussian blobs and a feature-based pipeline for joint detection, identification, and pose estimation. Each board is registered from hundreds of blob features whose dense spatial coverage constrains pose, while multiple scales preserve detectability across large changes in focal length, distance, and obliquity. Learned local descriptors are matched to the reference pattern and spatially verified, so the correspondences determine pose and certify identity. Against motion-capture ground truth, BlobBoards achieve median translation errors of 3.6-5.0 mm, reducing AprilTag's median translation error by 89% on small boards and 70% on large ones. They also produce far fewer large-rotation failures than state-of-the-art tag systems. BlobBoards achieve the highest detection rate, 80% versus 74% for AprilTag and 58% for ArUco, with the largest margin on the smallest markers. Under 50% occlusion, they still detect 69% of boards with essentially unchanged median translation error, while AprilTag and ArUco detect none. In experiments BlobBoards give state-of-the-art detection rate, pose accuracy and occlusion robustness.

</details>


### [39] [MWIR-4-Plastic: The Identification of Complex End-of-Life Industrial Plastic using Mid-wave Infrared Hyperspectral Imaging and Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28874)
*Elias Arbash,Andréa de Lima Ribeiro,Filipa Simões,Ahmed Jamal Afifi,Aldino Rizaldy,Yuleika Madriz,Samuel Thiele,Sandra Lorenz,Margret Fuchs,Pedram Ghamisi,Paul Scheunders,Richard Gloaguen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文首次公开了来自报废车辆工业废料中黑色碎塑料的HSI数据集，涵盖4种工业聚合物、13个共配准RGB/VNIR/SWIR/MWIR场景，并提出一个多模态光谱-空间框架，集成前景隔离、像素级分类和对象级多数投票，实现了复杂黑色塑料的准确分类，并基于9种处理方法建立了首个综合基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前回收设施中黑色碎塑料的自动化分选面临重大挑战：现有方法依赖单点接触式中红外光谱或实验室HSI，缺乏空间分辨分析能力；数据集多为实验室控制且未针对碎塑料；黑色工业塑料代表性不足；分类管线依赖人工区域选择和基于规则的频谱匹配，忽视了空间信息和现代深度学习方法。

Method: 构建了首个公开的黑色碎塑料HSI数据集（含RGB/VNIR/SWIR/MWIR共配准场景），开发了多模态光谱-空间框架，集成前景隔离、像素级分类和对象级多数投票；将地球观测领域的高级超光谱transformer进行适配，并结合化学计量学波段选择方法进行特征优化。

Result: 通过9种处理方法（涵盖化学计量学、机器学习和深度学习架构）建立了首个综合基准，实现了复杂黑色塑料的准确分类；数据集和方法论全部公开，确保可复现性。

Conclusion: 该研究填补了黑色碎塑料HSI数据集缺失和现代DL方法应用不足的空白，确立了工业检测中超光谱目标分析管线的基准，为回收设施中碎塑料的高效自动分选提供了可行的技术路径。

Abstract: The automated sorting of shredded black plastics from end-of-life (EOF) industrial waste presents a significant challenge in recycling facilities, primarily due to the limitations of current sensing and analytical approaches. Existing studies predominantly rely on single-point contact-based mid-infrared spectroscopy or laboratory hyperspectral imaging (HSI) setups, which fail to provide the spatially resolved analysis necessary for fast, bulk processing. Moreover, available datasets are laboratory-controlled and focus on intact rather than shredded plastics, hindering further recycling refinement. Black industrial plastics, in particular, are underrepresented, while most classification pipelines depend on manual region selection and rule-based spectral matching, neglecting spatial information and modern deep learning (DL) methods. To address these gaps, we introduce the first publicly available HSI dataset of shredded black plastics from EOF vehicle, comprising four industrial polymers across 13 co-registered RGB, VNIR, SWIR, and MWIR scenes and their segmentation pipeline. We developed a multi-modal spectral-spatial framework that integrates foreground isolation, pixel-wise classification, and object-level majority voting. By adapting advanced hyperspectral transformers from earth observation and incorporating chemometric band selection, we achieve accurate classification of complex black plastics. The study establishes the first comprehensive benchmark using nine processing methods, including chemometric, machine learning, and DL architectures. To ensure reproducibility, the complete dataset and methodologies are publicly released, establishing a benchmark for a hyperspectral object-analysis pipeline in industrial inspection.

</details>


### [40] [Pixel-wise Geo-registration of Drone and Satellite Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28891)
*Qingyang Liu,David G Shatwell,Parth Parag Kulkarni,Mubarak Shah*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了SkyReg数据集和基准，用于像素级无人机到卫星图像的地理配准任务，提供密集的逐像素地理坐标监督，并训练了几何感知重建管线，取得了显著的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有跨视角地理定位基准大多仅提供GPS标签，只能评估单坐标级的定位，缺乏对密集地理配准（逐像素级对齐）的探索和研究。

Method: 构建SkyReg数据集，涵盖正射和透视视角、多种场景类型（城市、地标中心、郊区/乡村）和相机配置，提供密集的逐像素地理定位监督；基于该数据集评估多种基线方法（检索、特征匹配、单应性对齐、前馈3D重建），并训练几何感知重建管线。

Result: 所提出的几何感知重建管线在SkyReg基准上取得了最先进的结果，性能提升显著。

Conclusion: SkyReg填补了密集像素级跨视角地理配准领域的空白，为后续研究提供了标准化基准，并验证了几何感知重建方法在提升配准精度方面的有效性。

Abstract: Pixel-level cross-view geo-registration aims to align a query image (e.g., drone) to a geo-referenced satellite map so that every query pixel can be mapped to real-world GPS coordinates. Despite strong progress in cross-view geo-localization, existing benchmarks largely provide only GPS labels, limiting evaluation to a single coordinate per image and leaving dense geodetic alignment underexplored. We introduce SkyReg, a dataset and standardized benchmark for pixel-level drone-to-satellite geo-registration, providing dense per-pixel geo-location supervision across diverse settings (orthographic and perspective), scene types (urban, landmark-centric, suburban/rural), and camera configurations. Using SkyReg, we evaluate a broad set of baselines spanning retrieval, feature matching, homography-based alignment, and feed-forward 3D reconstruction. Finally, cross-view pairs from SkyReg, we train a geometry-aware reconstruction pipeline that achieves state-of-the-art results,improving performance by a significant margin.

</details>


### [41] [ReconSplat: Generalizable 3D Scene Reconstruction Beyond Observed Views](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28895)
*Giuseppe Stracquadanio,Kevin Raj,Julia Grabinski,Stefan Roth*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ReconSplat是一个前馈式3D场景重建模型，通过3D高斯泼溅与多视角潜在扩散模型的整合，在未见区域的视图生成与几何一致性之间取得平衡，同时实现几何对齐的新视图生成和精确深度估计，并在RealEstate10K和DL3DV-10K基准上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决3D场景重建中长期存在的权衡问题：对未观测区域生成合理的视图与保持几何一致性之间的矛盾，现有方法难以兼顾视图生成质量与几何精度。

Method: 以3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）作为中间可微场景表示，将其与多视角潜在扩散模型（MV-LDM）整合。MV-LDM同时充当细化器和修复器，处理外观与场景几何。通过变分3D潜在特征引导扩散过程，这些特征由前馈式3DGS表示编码并光栅化到2D潜在空间，从而强制执行几何一致性。

Result: 在真实世界基准RealEstate10K和DL3DV-10K上，ReconSplat在具有挑战性的外推（extrapolation）设置中超越现有方法，能生成照片级真实的新视图和精确的深度图。

Conclusion: ReconSplat在生成未见视角时能够同时保持连贯而精确的场景几何，成功突破了传统方法在视图生成与几何一致性之间的瓶颈，展现出在高难度外推场景下的优越性能。

Abstract: We introduce ReconSplat, a feed-forward model for 3D scene reconstruction that aims to address the longstanding trade-off between plausible view generation for unobserved regions and geometric consistency, providing both geometrically aligned novel views and sharp depth estimates. Our approach builds on 3D Gaussian splatting (3DGS) as an intermediate differentiable scene representation and integrates it with a multi-view latent diffusion model (MV-LDM) trained to act simultaneously as a refiner and an inpainter for appearance and scene geometry. We enforce geometric consistency by guiding the diffusion process with variational 3D latent features for appearance and geometry, encoded by the feed-forward 3DGS representation and rasterized to 2D latent space. ReconSplat produces both photorealistic novel views and accurate depth maps on real-world benchmarks, RealEstate10K and DL3DV-10K, outperforming existing methods in challenging extrapolation setups. Notably, ReconSplat allows the extrapolation of unseen and challenging viewpoints jointly with coherent and precise scene geometry.

</details>


### [42] [Coarse to Fine: Iterative Adversarial Neural Cellular Automata for Medical Image Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28909)
*Anh Thi Luu,Nick Lemke,Anirban Mukhopadhyay*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出StyleGANCA，首个轻量级通用NCA生成对抗网络，将StyleGAN启发式映射网络与自适应风格调制集成到多尺度NCA合成过程中，实现潜在可控的图像生成。在BloodMNIST和PathMNIST上，以617k参数在PathMNIST上取得最佳FID和KID分数，图像质量可与基线方法媲美，且生成图像能有效支持多类分类器训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学影像领域受隐私和法律限制，大型公开数据集难以共享。合成数据生成提供隐私友好的替代方案，但现有最先进的生成模型虽然能生成高质量图像，却计算开销大，限制了在资源受限硬件上的应用。因此需要一种轻量级且高质量的生成模型。

Method: 提出StyleGANCA架构，将StyleGAN启发的映射网络和自适应风格调制集成到多尺度神经细胞自动机（NCA）合成过程中，通过迭代局部交互实现潜在可控的图像生成。在BloodMNIST和PathMNIST数据集上，与对抗、变分、扩散及NCA基线方法进行对比评估。

Result: StyleGANCA在PathMNIST上以仅617k参数取得最佳FID和KID分数，在BloodMNIST上也取得具有竞争力的图像质量，显著优于参数规模更大的基线架构。下游实验表明生成图像保留了类别特定信息，能有效支持多类分类器训练。

Conclusion: StyleGANCA证明了将NCA与StyleGAN风格机制相结合，能够在极低参数量的情况下生成高质量的医学图像，为资源受限硬件上的合成数据生成提供了实用方案，同时保持了图像的类别信息质量。

Abstract: Large-scale, publicly available datasets have driven advances in deep learning, but privacy and legal restrictions often limit data sharing in medical imaging. Synthetic data generation offers a privacy-friendly alternative to enable the training of high-performance models on health data. While most state-of-the-art generative models produce high-quality images, they remain computationally expensive, which limits their applicability on resource-constrained hardware. We propose StyleGANCA, the first lightweight general-purpose NCA-based generative adversarial network. The architecture integrates a StyleGAN-inspired mapping network and adaptive style modulation into a multi-scale NCA synthesis process, enabling latent-controlled image generation through iterative local interactions. We evaluate StyleGANCA on BloodMNIST and PathMNIST against adversarial, variational, diffusion, and NCA-based baselines. Experimental results demonstrate that StyleGANCA achieves competitive image quality with substantially fewer parameters than baseline architectures, achieving the best FID and KID scores on PathMNIST with only 617k parameters. Furthermore, downstream experiments show that the generated images preserve class-specific information and effectively support the training of multi-class classifiers. Our code is publicly available at: https://github.com/MECLabTUDA/StyleGANCA

</details>


### [43] [mmIR: Frequency-Space Inverse Rendering for 3D Millimeter-Wave Radar ADC Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28913)
*Adnan Armouti,Yixuan Gao,Rajalakshmi Nandakumar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: mmIR是一个开源的可微分FMCW雷达逆渲染器，利用LiDAR辅助的逆渲染技术，从真实雷达采集数据中拟合基于物理的前向模型，并通过密集虚拟孔径重渲染来合成高分辨率3D雷达数据，在ColoRadar场景上实现了0.914的Pearson相关性，显著优于Sionna-RT的0.307。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高分辨率3D雷达数据稀缺，商品级毫米波传感器的天线阵列小导致角分辨率受限，现有数据集仅提供2D距离-方位图或稀疏点云而非原始ADC信号。硬件扩展成本高，合成孔径扫描在车队规模不实用，而学习方法受限于数据短缺本身，因此需要一种能从物理模型中合成高分辨率雷达数据的方法。

Method: mmIR利用LiDAR辅助逆渲染：以LiDAR导出的网格作为几何支架，通过端到端自动微分优化每顶点ITU物理材质、顶点法线和天线波束模式，对相位相干的MIMO前向模型（包含多次反射传播、极化和自由空间衍射）进行物理建模，从真实采集拟合出前向模型参数，再从密集虚拟孔径重渲染合成高分辨率3D雷达数据。

Result: 在7个室外和6个室内ColoRadar场景上，mmIR在距离-方位图上达到0.914的平均Pearson相关性，远优于Sionna-RT的0.307。级联成像雷达训练的模型可无重训练迁移到共置单芯片雷达（相关性0.554），100x100元素的密集虚拟阵列能生成经LiDAR验证的单帧3D占用图。

Conclusion: mmIR证明基于物理的逆渲染方法可以有效弥补3D雷达数据短缺问题，通过LiDAR辅助的物理建模实现跨传感器迁移和高分辨率3D数据合成，为雷达数据生成提供了新的可行范式。

Abstract: High-resolution 3D radar data is scarce. Commodity mmWave sensors use small antenna arrays that limit angular resolution to several degrees, and existing datasets provide only 2D range-azimuth maps or sparse point clouds rather than raw analog-to-digital converter (ADC) signals. Hardware scaling is expensive, synthetic-aperture scanning is impractical at fleet scale, and learned synthesis methods are bottlenecked by the very data shortage they aim to address. We present mmIR, an open-source differentiable frequency-modulated continuous-wave (FMCW) radar inverse renderer that fits a physics-based forward model to real captures and re-renders from dense virtual apertures to synthesize high-resolution 3D radar data. Because radar resolution is too coarse to recover geometry directly, mmIR performs LiDAR-assisted inverse rendering: using LiDAR-derived meshes as a geometric scaffold, mmIR optimizes per-vertex International Telecommunication Union (ITU) physics materials, vertex normals, and antenna beam patterns through end-to-end automatic differentiation of a phase-coherent multiple-input multiple-output (MIMO) forward model with multi-bounce propagation, polarization, and free-space diffraction. On seven outdoor and six indoor ColoRadar scenes, mmIR achieves 0.914 mean Pearson correlation on range-azimuth maps versus 0.307 for Sionna-RT. Scenes trained on a cascaded imaging radar transfer to a co-located single-chip radar without re-training (0.554 correlation), and dense virtual arrays (100x100 elements) produce single-frame 3D occupancy validated against LiDAR. Project page: https://mmwave-inverse-rendering.github.io/

</details>


### [44] [ActiveAugment: Online Active Learning for Augmentation Selection in Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28923)
*Noah Videcrantz,Mostafa Mehdipour Ghazi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ActiveAugment，一种将数据增强选择视为在线主动学习问题的统一框架。该方法基于模型预测不确定性和特征差异对候选增强视图评分，为每个样本选择使当前模型最脆弱的增强方式，并联合使用监督分类和监督对比学习目标进行训练。在八个数据集（涵盖自然图像和医学图像）、多种架构和三种训练机制下，该方法优于AutoAugment、RandAugment和TrivialAugment，在低标注预算和医学图像场景下增益最为显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据增强策略是静态且与模型无关的预处理步骤，要么依赖昂贵的特定数据集策略搜索，要么随机均匀应用变换，未考虑模型已学到的内容。作者希望将增强选择与模型当前状态动态关联，实现更高效的自适应增强。

Method: ActiveAugment将增强选择建模为在线主动学习问题。每个训练小批次生成候选增强视图池，通过模型预测不确定性和增强引起的特征差异进行评分，为每个样本选择当前模型最脆弱的增强。训练目标结合了监督分类损失和监督对比损失，强制所选增强的类内不变性同时保持类间分离。框架评估涵盖八个主动选择策略及多种架构和训练机制。

Result: 在八个基准数据集（自然图像和医学图像）、CNN与Transformer架构、三种训练机制（从头训练、全量微调、线性探测）下，ActiveAugment在所有域和预算设置中均优于AutoAugment、RandAugment和TrivialAugment，在低标注预算下增益最大。在医学图像数据上（数据稀缺且与预训练模型域差异大），其测试F1高于所有基线，展现强跨域适应性。

Conclusion: ActiveAugment验证了动态、模型自适应的增强选择策略相较静态策略的优越性。增强选择策略在训练过程中会随模型状态有意义地演化，且策略选择直接影响泛化性能。这为数据增强从静态预处理向在线主动学习范式转变提供了有力证据。

Abstract: Data augmentation is a cornerstone of deep learning pipelines, yet existing strategies treat it as a static, model-agnostic preprocessing step, either relying on expensive dataset-specific policy search or applying transformations uniformly at random, regardless of what the model has already learned. We introduce ActiveAugment, a unified framework that treats augmentation selection as an online active learning problem. For each training minibatch, ActiveAugment generates a pool of candidate augmented views and scores each candidate using a combination of the model's predictive uncertainty and the feature discrepancy induced by the augmentation. The augmentation under which the current model is most fragile is selected per sample, and the model is then trained with a joint supervised classification and supervised contrastive objective that enforces intra-class invariance to the selected augmentations while maintaining inter-class separation. We evaluate ActiveAugment on eight benchmark datasets spanning natural and medical imaging, using CNN and transformer architectures across three training regimes (training from scratch, full fine-tuning, and linear probing), and comparing eight active selection strategies for augmentation scoring. ActiveAugment outperforms AutoAugment, RandAugment, and TrivialAugment under controlled augmentation shifts across all domains and budgets, with the most pronounced gains at low labelling budgets. On medical imaging datasets, where data is scarce and domain shift relative to natural-image pretrained models is large, ActiveAugment achieves higher test F1 than all baselines, demonstrating strong cross-domain adaptability. Our analysis reveals that the augmentation selection policy evolves meaningfully during training and that strategy choice has a direct impact on generalisation. Code is available at: https://github.com/noahvide/ActiveAugment.

</details>

*Vage Taamazyan,Zhuowen Shen,Stefan Hinterstoisser,Alberto Dall'Olio,Agastya Kalra,Aarrushi Shandilya,Xin Li,Wenping Wang,Kartik Venkataraman*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文挑战了立体匹配任务中对架构归纳偏置的传统依赖，证明一个完全无归纳偏置的端到端Vision Transformer，仅通过在海量合成数据上训练，即可在准确性和运行效率上超越所有现有方法，解锁3D重建的缩放定律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 立体匹配是目前最后一个仍依赖重型架构归纳偏置的计算机视觉任务，尽管已有研究表明通用方法可以解决该任务，但学界普遍认为归纳偏置对高质量结果和计算效率是严格必要的。作者希望打破这一范式。

Method: 采用一个完全不含架构归纳偏置的简单端到端Vision Transformer，在海量合成数据集上进行训练，通过纯数据驱动学习而非显式几何约束来完成立体匹配。

Result: 该方法在达到最先进准确率的同时，实现了更优的运行时效率，证明了纯数据驱动学习可以超越显式工程化的几何方法。

Conclusion: 显式归纳偏置不再是立体匹配的先决条件，纯数据驱动方法能够解锁3D重建中持续改进的真正缩放定律。

Abstract: Stereo reconstruction is one of the last remaining Computer Vision tasks where all state-of-the-art methods employ a heavy architectural inductive bias. Even though it has been demonstrated that the task can be solved using general-purpose methods, it is widely believed that inductive biases in stereo are strictly necessary for both high-quality results and computational efficiency. We challenge this paradigm. In this paper, we demonstrate that both state-of-the-art accuracy and superior runtime efficiency are achievable with a model completely devoid of architectural inductive biases, relying instead on a simple, end-to-end Vision Transformer. By training on massive synthetic datasets, we show that pure data-driven learning can surpass explicitly engineered geometry. This work proves that explicit inductive biases are no longer a prerequisite for stereo matching, ultimately unlocking true scaling laws for continuous improvement in 3D reconstruction.

</details>


### [46] [FractureFields: Contact-Aware Binary Multi-Field Transfer for Fractured 3D Gaussian Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28982)
*Jianchen Wang,Runyang Qu,Fei Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出FractureFields，一种拓扑自适应的传输方法，用于断裂3D高斯物体的模拟。通过为每个断裂碎片构建独立的欧拉场并在G2P更新中进行场感知路由，消除跨碎片动量泄漏，同时引入动量守恒的接触投影来处理再接触。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的Gaussia-MPM管线即使在断裂后仍保持单一欧拉速度场，导致断裂碎片在共享插值支持时发生跨碎片动量泄漏，表现为残余粘附和非物理拉伸。

Method: 1) 结构事件后为每个碎片分配持久身份标识；2) 在单次P2G传递中构建碎片特定的质量和动量场；3) 独立推进每个场；4) 执行场感知的G2P更新，使粒子仅采样自己碎片的网格状态；5) 添加动量守恒的接触投影，仅在两个碎片场接近时施加等大反向法向冲量。

Result: 在重建场景和受控再接触基准测试中，碎片条件路由通过构造消除了已实现的跨碎片混合，接触投影在碰撞期间减少相互穿透且不重新引入残余耦合。

Conclusion: 断裂后的模拟应将结构断开视为局部动力学状态的改变，而不仅仅是本构应力的改变。

Abstract: Physics-integrated 3D Gaussian representations make it possible to simulate image-reconstructed assets directly as particles, but current Gaussia-MPM pipelines keep a single Eulerian velocity field even after fracture. When disconnected fragments share interpolation support, they still write to and read from the same grid nodes, producing cross-fragment momentum leakage that appears as residual adhesion and non-physical stretching. We present FractureFields, a topology-adaptive transfer for fractured 3D Gaussian objects. After a structural event assigns persistent fragment identities, FractureFields builds fragment-specific mass and momentum fields in a single P2G pass, advances each field independently, and performs a field-aware G2P update so particles only sample their own fragment's grid state. To handle re-contact, we add a momentum-conserving contact projection that applies equal and opposite normal impulses only when two fragment fields are approaching, preserving free separation otherwise. Experiments on reconstructed scenes and a controlled re-contact benchmark show that fragment-conditioned routing eliminates realized cross-fragment mixing by construction, while contact projection reduces interpenetration during collision without reintroducing residual coupling. Overall, we argue that post-fracture simulation should treat structural disconnection as a change in local dynamical state, not merely a change in constitutive stress.

</details>


### [47] [RoSe-SLAM: Robust Semantic-Aware Gaussian Splatting SLAM from Dynamic Monocular Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29003)
*Wenting Wang,Jiaxin Guo,Wenzhen Dong,Yun-Hui Liu,Charlie C. L. Wang,Yeung Yam*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出RoSe-SLAM，一种利用2D基础模型语义特征进行动态场景理解的鲁棒语义感知高斯溅射SLAM系统，通过空间-时间运动掩码生成和遮挡感知关键帧选择机制，实现动态环境下的精确相机追踪与高质量静态场景重建，在TUM、Bonn和Wild-Mocap数据集上优于现有动态RGB SLAM基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统SLAM系统基于静态假设，在动态、非结构化环境中会遭遇显著的精度退化，难以有效处理动态物体与静态背景的分离问题。

Method: 1) 利用2D基础模型的语义特征蒸馏到高斯场，实现语义感知的多视图一致性；2) 提出空间-时间运动掩码生成模块，同时捕捉长期运动监控和短期瞬态动态；3) 在全局束调整中提出遮挡感知关键帧选择机制和多元语义一致性模块，动态过滤不可靠观测并重建静态场景几何。

Result: 在动态TUM、Bonn和Wild-Mocap等基准数据集上进行的大量实验表明，所提方法在轨迹估计和静态场景映射方面均取得优越性能，在长期动态室内环境中优于现有动态RGB SLAM基线。

Conclusion: 结合几何运动线索与语义先验信息，RoSe-SLAM能够有效解耦动态物体与静态背景，实现动态环境下高精度的相机追踪和高品质静态场景重建，为该领域提供了新的解决范式。

Abstract: In dynamic and unstructured environments, conventional SLAM systems generally suffer from significant accuracy degeneration due to their static assumptions. In this work, we propose Robust Semantic-aware Gaussian Splatting SLAM (RoSe-SLAM), to address the dynamic challenge by a holistic semantic scene understanding from uncalibrated monocular inputs, achieving accurate camera tracking and high-quality geometry reconstruction. Unlike conventional semantic SLAM using handcrafted semantic labels, our RoSe-SLAM exploits the semantic feature from 2D foundation model to enhance the dynamic tracking and mapping performance. By distilling the rich semantic features to our Gaussian fields, our method effectively identifies dynamic distractors and achieves semantic-aware multi-view consistency, significantly enhancing the geometric reconstruction and scene inpainting. Specifically, we propose a spatial-temporal motion mask generation module, enabling both long-term motion monitoring and short-term transient dynamics capturing, achieving robust and effective disentanglement of dynamic objects and static backgrounds. During global bundle adjustment, we propose an occlusion-aware keyframe selection mechanism to prioritize the occlusion as metric to pick the keyframes, and a multi-view semantic consistency module to improve the mapping quality in dynamic environments. By combining geometric motion cues with semantic priors, our system dynamically filters unreliable observations and reconstructs accurate static scene geometry. Extensive experiments conducted on benchmark datasets including dynamic TUM, Bonn and Wild-Mocap datasets, demonstrate that our method achieves superior performance in both trajectory estimation and static scene mapping, outperforming existing dynamic RGB SLAM baselines in long-term dynamic indoor environments.

</details>


### [48] [Towards Fully Automated Medical Imaging Code Generation via Validation-based Context Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29016)
*Zixiao Zhao,Jing Sun,Zhe Hou,Cheng-Hao Cai,Qian Liu,Mengze Li,Zijian Zhang,Jin Song Dong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AutoMedImg是一个面向医学图像处理代码生成的多智能体框架，通过规划阶段与编码阶段的协同以及多级验证机制，实现零人工干预的自动化流程生成，在多个医学影像数据集上取得了优异性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用大语言模型在程序生成方面已有显著进展，但在医学图像处理等复杂领域任务中存在明显局限：缺乏领域知识、缺少可靠的验证机制，往往需要大量人工干预才能生成可用的处理流程。本文旨在解决这一痛点。

Method: AutoMedImg采用两阶段多智能体架构：规划阶段负责数据集分析与架构设计，结合语义及形式化验证；编码阶段并行生成模块，并执行静态检查、运行测试和组装验证。此外引入自动上下文工程（领域知识库+共享内存+验证反馈）和跨项目自适应流水线合成机制，实现流水线的积累与复用。

Result: 在六个医学影像数据集上使用五种骨干大语言模型进行评估，AutoMedImg实现了零人工干预，分割任务Dice分数最高达0.90，分类任务准确率最高达99%。

Conclusion: AutoMedImg通过多智能体协作、多阶段验证和跨项目学习，有效解决了通用LLM在医学图像处理代码生成中的领域知识与验证不足问题，验证了全自动生成医学图像处理管线的可行性与高效性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated considerable promise in program generation for small-scale and conventional application development; however, they remain limited when applied to complex, domain-specific tasks such as medical image processing. General-purpose models lack explicit domain knowledge and robust validation mechanisms to ensure correctness, often requiring substantial human intervention to produce reliable processing pipelines. To address these limitations, we propose AutoMedImg, a multi-agent framework for fully automated medical image processing code generation. AutoMedImg orchestrates specialised agents across two phases: a Planning Phase that performs dataset analysis and architecture design with semantic and formal verification, and a Coding Phase that generates modules in parallel with static checking, execution testing, and assembly validation. This multi-stage validation mitigates error propagation throughout generation, while comprehensive auto-context engineering combining domain-specific knowledge bases, shared memory, and validation feedback automates context construction without manual prompting. A cross-project adaptive pipeline synthesis mechanism further accumulates validated pipelines and retrieves proven components for new tasks based on project similarity, enhancing generation efficiency through cross-project learning. Extensive evaluation across six diverse and well-established medical imaging datasets with five backbone LLMs demonstrates that AutoMedImg achieves zero human intervention, with Dice scores of up to 0.90 for segmentation tasks and 99% accuracy for classification.

</details>


### [49] [Explainable Multi-Loss Distillation Framework for Efficient and Interpretable Shrimp Disease Text Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29027)
*Anh Nguyen Quynh,Khang Nguyen Quoc,Luyl-Da Quach*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了SALT框架，一种基于知识蒸馏的虾病文本分类方法，结合LIME和SHAP可解释性分析，在多个蒸馏目标下取得优于监督基线的性能，同时兼顾计算效率与可解释性，为早期虾病诊断提供了新思路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虾病分类对越南等水产出口国经济至关重要，但现有研究多依赖图像分类，只能在疾病显现晚期进行检测，难以实现早期诊断。因此需要基于文本的分类方法来实现及时、早期的疾病检测。

Method: 提出SALT框架，采用多损失知识蒸馏（multi-loss distillation）进行文本分类，并引入LIME和SHAP两种可解释性分析方法，用于评估模型预测效果并解读学习到的语言特征。

Result: 实验表明SALT在多个蒸馏目标下均取得有竞争力的性能，优于有监督基线，在预测性能与计算效率之间实现了良好平衡。同时展现出强可解释性，能准确识别与疾病描述相关的关键语言特征和语义模式。

Conclusion: 基于知识蒸馏的文本分类方法在早期虾病诊断及相关研究方向具有广阔应用前景，为水产疾病监测提供了高效且可解释的技术路径。

Abstract: Shrimp disease classification has become an urgent issue due to its significant impact on the import-export output of producing countries, particularly Vietnam. Most existing studies focus on image-based classification, which typically operates at the late stage of disease manifestation. Therefore, text-based classification has the potential to enable early and timely disease detection. To address this limitation, we introduce the SALT (Shrimp disease text Analysis with multi-Loss disTillation) framework, which incorporates explainability analysis using Local Interpretable Model-agnostic Explanations (LIME) and SHapley Additive exPlanations (SHAP) to evaluate model predictions and interpret the learned linguistic features. Experimental results demonstrate that SALT achieves competitive performance across multiple distillation objectives, outperforming supervised baselines while providing a favorable trade-off between predictive performance and computational efficiency. Moreover, it exhibits strong explainability, accurately identifying key linguistic features and semantic patterns relevant to disease descriptions. These findings highlight the potential of knowledge distillation-based text classification for future applications in early shrimp disease diagnosis and related research directions.

</details>


### [50] [DocIntent: Answerability-Guided Agentic Restoration for Real-World Document Visual Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29037)
*Zihan Huang,Shihang Wu,Junle Liu,Peirong Zhang,Yongxin Shi,Xuhan Zheng,Lianwen Jin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DocIntent，一个无需训练的基于可回答性引导的智能体修复框架，用于真实世界退化文档的视觉问答任务。该框架通过评估问题可回答性、识别任务相关退化并选择性调用修复工具，同时利用基于比较的回滚机制验证每次修复步骤，有效提升了多种MLLM在WildDoc基准上的平均得分和一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界中的退化（如模糊、阴影、失真和摩尔纹）严重损害了多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）的文档问答能力。虽然修复工具是直观的解决方案，但现有方法需要人工设计策略且依赖领域专业知识；而现有的智能体修复框架主要针对自然图像，追求感知质量而非服务于下游任务。

Method: DocIntent框架包含三个核心环节：(1) 评估问题可回答性以决策是否需要修复；(2) 识别任务相关的退化类型并选择性调用相应的修复工具；(3) 通过基于比较的回滚机制验证每个修复步骤，当问题相关证据变得难以辨认时撤销该步骤。整个框架无需训练，也不需要额外的退化分类器或图像质量评估模型。

Result: 在WildDoc基准上的大量实验表明，DocIntent能够持续提升不同开源和闭源MLLMs的平均得分和回答一致性。

Conclusion: 智能体修复应以服务下游任务为导向，而非追求通用的图像质量指标。DocIntent通过可回答性引导和回滚验证机制，在无需额外训练模型的情况下有效提升了退化文档的VQA性能。

Abstract: Real-world degradations such as blur, shadow, distortion, and moire patterns severely impair the document question-answering capabilities of Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs). Applying restoration tools before Visual Question Answering (VQA) is an intuitive solution. However, existing restoration approaches remain limited, as manually designing and executing restoration strategies is labor-intensive and requires domain expertise. Agentic restoration offers new possibilities for automation, yet existing frameworks primarily target natural images and pursue perceptual quality, overlooking that restoration should serve downstream tasks rather than optimize generic image quality metrics. To this end, we explore the value of agentic restoration for real-world degraded document VQA and propose DocIntent, a training-free Answerability-Guided Agentic Restoration framework. DocIntent first assesses question answerability, then identifies task-relevant degradations and selectively invokes restoration tools. A Comparison-Based Rollback mechanism validates each restoration step and reverts it when question-relevant evidence becomes less decipherable. The entire process requires no additional pretrained degradation classifier or image quality assessment model. Extensive experiments on the WildDoc benchmark show that DocIntent consistently improves the average score and consistency of different open- and closed-source MLLMs. The code and experimental data will be publicly available.

</details>

### [51] [RedLight-VLA: Models for traffic-rule grounding and behavioral emphasis in driving policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28656)
*Bala Murali Manoghar Sai Sudhakar,Sourab Bapu Sridhar,Sandipan Das,Rahul Ahuja,Meda Lazar,Ashish Garg,Pratik Likhar,Senthil Yogamani*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出RedLight-VLA，一种针对行为克隆VLA驾驶策略的训练目标，通过轨迹导出的行为重加权（BR）和并行辅助头（AUX）来改善信号交叉口的规则驾驶行为，无需额外人工规则标注。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 行为克隆的VLA驾驶策略在信号交叉口处理稀有规则驾驶动作（如刹车和起步）时表现不佳，因为这类样本在平均轨迹损失中贡献极小，且融合表征缺乏对交通灯和停止线状态的显式监督。

Method: 提出两种互补机制：(1) 轨迹导出的行为重加权（BR），利用旋转不变的纵向动力学和保尺度缩减来强调稀有减速和加速样本，禁用时可精确恢复基线；(2) 并行辅助头（AUX），在融合后规则令牌上连续监督交通灯和停止线状态，无需自回归语言生成或修改轨迹解码器。

Result: 在20秒序列、5秒预测窗口的评估中，相比相同VLA基线，RedLight-VLA将红灯停止线超调从7.3%降至6.8%，停止线速度误差降低12.7%，3秒交通灯切片ADE/FDE从0.274/0.964m改善至0.247/0.897m。绿灯误停略有增加（3.2%→3.9%），但BR+AUX组合缓解了AUX单独的更大增幅；组合模型还改善非交通灯场景ADE/FDE。

Conclusion: BR与AUX的组合在交通灯和非交通灯场景中均优于任一单独机制，有效提升了信号交叉口的规则驾驶能力，且无需额外人工规则标注。

Abstract: Behavior-cloned Vision-Language-Action (VLA) driving policies struggle with rare rule-governed maneuvers at signalized intersections. Braking and launching examples contribute little to averaged trajectory loss, while fused representations lack explicit supervision for the governing traffic-light and stop-line state. We present RedLight-VLA, a training objective that uses expert futures and automatically generated perception targets without additional manual rule annotation. First, trajectory-derived behavioral reweighting (BR) emphasizes rare deceleration and acceleration using rotation-invariant longitudinal dynamics and a scale-preserving reduction that exactly recovers the baseline when disabled. Second, parallel auxiliary (AUX) heads ground traffic-light and stop-line state in continuous post-fusion rule tokens, without autoregressive language generation or changes to the trajectory decoder. We evaluate on a curated set of 20 s sequences with a 5 s prediction horizon. Controlled variants share the same backbone, training data, decoder, and evaluation population. Against an otherwise identical VLA baseline, RedLight-VLA reduces red-light stop-line overshoot from 7.3% to6.8%, reduces stop-line velocity error by 12.7%, and improves 3 s trafficlight-sliced ADE/FDE from 0.274/0.964 m to 0.247/0.897 m. Green-light false stops increase from 3.2% to 3.9%; however, combining BR with AUX supervision mitigates the larger increase observed for AUX alone (4.0%). The combined model also improves non-traffic-light ADE/FDE from 0.268/0.956 m to 0.241/0.876 m and outperforms either mechanism alone on all four sliced displacement measures.

</details>


### [52] [The Potential of Haptic Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28664)
*Jianquan Wang,Haiwei Dong,Abdulmotaleb El Saddik*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文探讨了触觉基础模型（HFMs）的变革潜力与发展路径，旨在克服当前触觉感知模型因硬件异构性和物理数据采集困难而固化为任务特定模型的局限，提出了从被动语言/视觉模型向主动触觉基础模型转变的范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型在语言和视觉领域已取得巨大成功，但在具身AI中因缺乏通用触觉感知而受到瓶颈限制，尤其在消费电子领域（智能手机、可穿戴设备、VR控制器、家庭机器人、健康监测等）需要安全且自适应的物理交互。当前触觉模型受限于硬件异构性和主动物理数据采集的必要性，导致模型僵化且仅能处理特定任务。

Method: 论文提出了从被动LLM和VLM向主动HFMs转变的范式，涵盖四个核心维度：动作耦合、物理动态表示空间、连续时间序列数据粒度、以及动作条件下的未来状态预测。同时综合了现有的大规模触觉数据集，并在TacBench基准上对UniTouch、AnyTouch、T3和Sparsh进行了力估计、滑移检测和相对位姿估计的评估。

Result: 在TacBench基准上，对UniTouch、AnyTouch、T3和Sparsh四个代表性触觉模型进行了系统评估，涵盖力估计、滑移检测和相对位姿估计三个任务。

Conclusion: 触觉基础模型是突破具身AI触觉感知瓶颈的关键方向，需要从被动感知向主动交互的范式转变，并通过大规模数据集与统一基准来推动该领域的发展。

Abstract: Despite the success of foundation models in language and vision, their expansion into embodied AI is bottlenecked by a lack of generalized touch sensing. This limitation is especially relevant to consumer electronics, where smartphones, wearables, VR controllers, home robots, and health monitoring devices require safe and adaptive physical interaction. Constrained by hardware heterogeneity and the necessity of active physical data collection, current haptic models remain rigidly task-specific. To overcome these limitations, this article explores the transformative potential and developmental trajectory of Haptic Foundation Models (HFMs). We detail the paradigm shift required to transition from passive Large Language Models and Vision Language Models into active HFMs across four core dimensions: action coupling, physical dynamical representation space, continuous time-series data granularity, and action-conditioned future state prediction. Furthermore, we synthesize existing large-scale tactile datasets and benchmark UniTouch, AnyTouch, T3, and Sparsh on TacBench for force estimation, slip detection, and relative pose estimation.

</details>


### [53] [Cognitively-Grounded On-Device Runtime Learning for Ground Robots in Unknown Physical Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28677)
*Yihao Cai,Yanbing Mao,Christian Lebiere*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出CogRun框架，使安全关键的地面机器人在无先验地图和感知知识的未知物理环境中，完全在边缘AI设备上执行认知驱动的运行时学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人在未知环境中运行时学习面临安全性与效率的挑战，且依赖无线通信和先验知识，限制了在无连接或连接受限环境中的广泛应用。

Method: CogRun由三个组件构成：Learning-Agent采用新颖的认知神经学习架构，具备专用回放缓冲区、认知驱动的经验采样以及演员-评论家强化学习与实例学习的安全感知动作融合；Rational-Agent作为非学习模块专门处理安全关键功能；Coordinator管理两智能体间的交互以促进安全高效的运行时学习。

Result: 在真实野外森林的四足机器人和模拟野外森林的越野自动驾驶车辆上的实验表明，CogRun可实现安全高效的运行时学习，使机器人能够在复杂未知环境中安全且持续地与物理世界交互以提升任务表现。

Conclusion: CogRun通过在边缘AI设备上部署完整的感知、学习与控制自主栈，消除了对无线通信的依赖，为有限或无连接环境中的安全关键机器人提供了一种可行的运行时学习方案。

Abstract: This paper presents \ul{CogRun}, a framework that enables safety-critical ground robots to perform cognitively-grounded runtime learning entirely on edge-AI devices in unknown physical environments, without prior maps or perceptual knowledge. CogRun consists of three components: a Learning-Agent, a Rational-Agent, and a Coordinator. The Learning-Agent is novel in cognitive-neural learning architecture, which featurs dedicated replay buffers, cognition-driven experience sampling, and a safety-aware action blending of actor-critic reinforcement learning (RL) with instance-based learning (IBL). The Rational-Agent is a non-learning module that complements the Learning-Agent by exclusively handling safety-critical functions, while the Coordinator manages interactions between the two agents to promote safe and efficient runtime learning. CogRun's full autonomy stack (i.e., perception, learning, and control) on edge-AI devices eliminates dependence on wireless communications, enabling broader applications in challenging environments with limited or no connectivity. Experiments on a quadruped robot in real-world wild forests and on an off-road autonomous vehicle in a simulated wild forest demonstrate that CogRun enables safe and efficient runtime learning, allowing robots to safely and continuously interact with the physical world for enhancing task performance in complex, unknown environments.

</details>


### [54] [RoboGesture: Real-Time Semantic-aligned Co-Speech Gestures Generation for Humanoid Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28693)
*Zifan Wang,Ziang Ren,Pengyang Shi,Zirui Wang,Chenghuai Lin,Tianze Wang,Zekun Qi,Liangliang Zhao,He Wang,Li Yi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoboGesture是一个面向人形机器人的端到端手势生成框架，通过协同设计数据集、模型和控制策略，实现机器人对语音的实时、有节奏且语义匹配的手势响应。该系统包含超过300类手势的RoboGesture数据集、层次化语义-声学对齐器、基于扩散Transformer的流式运动生成器、Anti-Inertia CFG Masking机制，以及MPC安全过滤器，在Unitree G1人形机器人上验证了优越性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人需要能够对人类语音做出同步且语义有意义的手势响应以实现自然的人机交互，但现有方法面临三大挑战：语义丰富数据集稀缺、"模态遮蔽"问题（模型忽视音频线索而依赖运动惯性）、以及物理安全方面的模拟到现实鸿沟。

Method: RoboGesture采用数据-建模-控制协同设计的思路：1) 构建包含300+手势类别的数据集，并开发自动化流水线合成大规模无碰撞、机器人特定的音频-运动对；2) 提出层次化语义-声学对齐器，从原始音频token中提取多粒度的韵律和语义线索；3) 使用基于扩散Transformer和条件流匹配的流式条件运动生成器；4) 引入Anti-Inertia CFG Masking防止模型陷入历史重复模式，迫使模型主动从音频模态挖掘控制信号；5) 采用MPC安全过滤器确保实时无碰撞的物理执行。

Result: 在Unitree G1人形机器人上的实验表明，与最先进的基线方法相比，RoboGesture能够生成更安全、更有节奏感、语义更恰当的手势响应。

Conclusion: RoboGesture通过数据、模型和控制的协同设计，成功解决了人形机器人手势生成中的数据集稀缺、模态遮蔽和sim-to-real安全问题，为构建完整的实时交互人形机器人系统提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Enabling humanoid robots to respond to human speech with synchronized and semantically meaningful gestures is fundamental to natural human-robot interaction. However, this task faces three critical barriers: the scarcity of semantically rich datasets, the "modality eclipse" where models ignore audio cues in favor of kinematic inertia, and the sim-to-real gap regarding physical safety. We propose RoboGesture, a robot-centric framework that co-designs data, modeling, and control to power a complete interactive human-humanoid system in which the robot listens, responds, and gestures in real time. We first establish the RoboGesture dataset featuring over 300 gesture categories and develop an automated pipeline to synthesize large-scale collision-free, robot-specific audio-motion pairs. Our architecture features a Hierarchical Semantic-Acoustic Aligner that extracts multi-granular prosodic and semantic cues directly from raw audio tokens. These cues drive a Streaming Conditional Motion Generator based on a diffusion transformer with conditional flow matching. To ensure high responsiveness, we introduce Anti-Inertia CFG Masking, which prevents the model from collapsing into repetitive historical patterns by compelling it to proactively mine control signals from the audio modality. Finally, an MPC-based safety filter ensures real-time, collision-free execution on physical hardware. Experiments on a Unitree G1 humanoid demonstrate that RoboGesture generates safer, more rhythmic, and more semantically appropriate responses compared to state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


### [55] [Multi-Group Pipe Routing under Permanent Geometric Occupancy: Problem, Benchmark, and Classical Baselines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28697)
*Deng Quan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究了增材制造中多组管道永久几何占用下的路由问题，提出了问题形式化、三维基准测试套件以及经典算法的基线结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 增材制造使得液压元件内部流体通道可以沿任意三维路径布置，但通道一旦放置就永久占据空间，后续通道必须避开已有几何，这与经典多智能体路径规划（MAPF）中智能体移动后释放空间不同，现有方法无法直接适用，需要新的问题定义和基准。

Method: (1) 提出了多组管道路由问题的形式化定义，引入了几何对偶见证冲突的概念；(2) 构建了可复现的三维基准测试套件，包含两种走廊生成器、两种障碍绘制器，并控制难度并提供可行性见证；(3) 将CBS、PBS和优先级规划三种经典算法适配到该耦合场景作为基线。

Result: 在PlaneSlice困难实例中，CBS明显优于PBS和优先级规划，而在较简单实例中三种方法均能验证流程有效性。算法性能通过固定时间预算下的成功率进行评估。

Conclusion: 本文目标是提供可复现的问题定义、基准测试套件和经典基线方法，而非提出新的最优MAPF算法，为后续研究该问题的算法打下基础。

Abstract: Additive manufacturing (AM) enables compact hydraulic components whose internal fluid channels can follow free-form 3D paths rather than conventionally drilled holes. A representative case is multi-group channel layout in rotary direct-drive servo valves: once a channel is placed it permanently occupies volume, so later channels must clear earlier geometry--unlike classical multi-agent pathfinding (MAPF), where agents free space after moving. We study this setting as multi-group pipe routing under permanent geometric occupancy. Our contributions are a problem formalization with geometric dual-witness conflicts, a constructive 3D benchmark (two corridor generators x two obstacle painters, controlled difficulty, feasibility witnesses), and baseline results for CBS, PBS, and priority planning adapted to this coupling. We evaluate by success rate under a fixed time budget. PlaneSlice hard instances clearly rank CBS above PBS and PP, while easier cells validate the pipeline. The goal is a reproducible problem definition, suite, and classical baselines--not a new optimal MAPF algorithm.

</details>


### [56] [RoboPhys-3D: A Comprehensive Embodied World Model Evaluation via 3D Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28718)
*Tianyi Wang,Jiazhou Chen,Yiming Xu,Xiangyu Li,Tianyi Zeng,Chih-Hsien Chou,Ning Lu,Liang Peng,Junfeng Jiao,Christian Claudel*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出RoboPhys-3D，一个基于3D重建协议的具身世界模型基准，包含50个操作任务、5000个片段、25000个多视角视频，并通过统一的3D重建管线区分重建误差与生成误差，引入50个指标、18个子维度和两级评分体系进行综合评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有具身世界模型基准缺乏统一的3D接地协议，无法验证生成的视频是否保持底层3D场景状态或能否转化为可执行动作，导致评估结果不可靠。

Method: 基于RoboTwin 2.0构建50个操作任务基准，生成视频与真实视频通过相同的3D重建管线处理以区分误差来源；设计50个互补指标、18个子维度、4个评估层级（像素保真度、3D几何一致性、状态级理解、任务级完整性），并引入Average Full Score和RoboPhyscore两种评分机制。

Result: 四个代表性视频世界模型中，Cosmos 3取得最高RoboPhyscore（0.6330，为真实值92.7%），但状态级和执行级指标揭示其存在显著失败；RoboPhyscore与人类评估高度一致（Pearson r=0.9761，Spearman ρ=0.8962）。

Conclusion: 基于3D接地和执行感知的评估对具身世界模型能力评测至关重要，传统感知级和视觉语言模型判断无法捕捉的失败可通过RoboPhys-3D有效识别。

Abstract: Video world models increasingly serve as data engines, action planners, and simulators for embodied AI, but conventional embodied world model (EWM) benchmarks lack a unified 3D-grounded protocol for establishing whether generated rollouts preserve the underlying 3D scene state or translate into executable actions. We introduce RoboPhys-3D, a 3D-grounded EWM benchmark built on RoboTwin 2.0, covering 50 manipulation tasks across four regimes, with 5,000 episodes and 25,000 multi-view ground-truth videos. A defining feature of RoboPhys-3D is that generated and ground-truth videos are processed through the same 3D reconstruction pipeline, enabling reconstruction-induced error to be distinguished from generation-induced error. The RoboPhys-3D benchmark organizes 50 complementary metrics into 18 sub-dimensions across four levels: pixel-level fidelity, 3D geometry consistency, state-level understanding, and task-level completeness. We further introduce Average Full Score, a hierarchical score averaging all 50 metrics for comprehensive evaluation, and RoboPhyscore, a compact task-aligned score averaging the metrics most strongly correlated with task success. Among the four representative video world models, Cosmos 3 achieves the highest RoboPhyscore (0.6330, 92.7% of ground truth), while state- and execution-grounded metrics reveal substantial failures that perceptual and vision-language model-based judgments fail to capture. RoboPhyscore further exhibits strong agreement with human evaluation (Pearson r = 0.9761 and Spearman \r{ho} = 0.8962), demonstrating the importance of grounded, execution-aware evaluation for EWM capability.

</details>


### [57] [Vehicle Drift Emergence: Continuous Evolution from Grip Driving to the Handling Limit via Boundary Exploration Learning Model Predictive Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28723)
*Sheng Zhao,Binh-Minh Nguyen,Hangyu Lu,Xiaodong Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出边界探索学习模型预测控制（BE-LMPC）方法，在无显式漂移参考的迭代圈时最小化任务中，通过构建经验安全集和局部偏移终端代价，使控制器在提升圈速过程中自发涌现漂移行为。实验表明在低摩擦系数（0.6）下漂移在Lap 11首次出现，圈时从49.95s降至25.50s，而高摩擦系数下无漂移产生，说明漂移是极限操控的条件性延续而非独立预设的运动模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动驾驶漂移控制器通常预设漂移平衡点、侧偏角参考或轨迹，关注如何执行漂移，而忽略了从抓地行驶到漂移之间的连续过渡问题尚未解决。作者希望在无显式漂移参考的条件下，探究漂移如何作为极限操控的自然延伸而涌现。

Method: 提出边界探索学习模型预测控制（BE-LMPC），从已完成圈次构建经验安全集和局部偏移终端代价，在固定全局速度限制下迭代优化空间速度分配，逐步探索更大的侧偏角和横摆角速度包络，同时保持可恢复性。

Result: 在摩擦系数0.6时，圈时从Lap 3的49.95s降至Lap 12的25.50s，漂移首次出现在Lap 11，达到16.5°侧偏角和0.894后轴利用率；而在摩擦系数0.8至1.2时无漂移产生，1.2时达到相似峰值速度但后轴利用率仅为0.483。通过组合滑移Fiala模型的连续性分析证明轮胎力从附着到滑移过渡不存在突变。

Conclusion: 漂移是当性能需求接近轮胎可用能力时条件性延续极限操控的自然涌现结果，而非预先设定的独立运动模式。连续过渡条件确保了从抓地到漂移的平滑转变。

Abstract: Automated drift controllers commonly track a prescribed drift equilibrium, sideslip reference, or trajectory. These formulations establish how to execute drift, whereas the continuous transition from grip driving to drift near the handling limit remains unresolved. This paper defines drift emergence in a repetitive lap time minimization task, where neither the controller objective nor the reward contains an explicit drift reference. A boundary exploration learning model predictive controller (BE-LMPC) constructs an empirical safe set and a locally shifted terminal cost from completed laps. By iteratively improving spatial speed allocation under a fixed global speed bound, the controller progressively explores larger sideslip and yaw rate envelopes while preserving recoverability. As lap performance improves, sustained sideslip and pronounced yaw motion emerge while the rear axle approaches saturation. Analysis shows that, when external conditions vary smoothly, the transition from tire adhesion to sliding does not itself cause abrupt changes in tire force or vehicle state. The combined-slip Fiala model satisfies this continuity condition at the transition. At a tire road friction coefficient of 0.6, lap time decreases from 49.95 s on Lap~3 to 25.50 s on Lap~12, with drift first emerging on Lap~11. Lap~12 reaches 16.5$^\circ$ sideslip and 0.894 rear axle utilization. In contrast, no drift is detected for friction coefficients from 0.8 to 1.2; at 1.2, a similar peak speed is achieved with only 0.483 rear axle utilization. These results characterize drift as a conditional continuation of limit handling that emerges when increasing performance demand approaches the available tire capacity, rather than as a separately prescribed motion mode.

</details>


### [58] [Generation of High-Level Concepts in 3D Scene Graphs via Autoregressive Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28733)
*Jose Andres Millan-Romera,Samuel Cognolato,Holger Voos,Jose Luis Sanchez-Lopez,Luciano Serafini*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种统一的自回归扩散图生成模型，用于联合学习3D场景图的结构和空间节点特征，从观测到的垂直平面自底向上构建任意层次深度的完整室内3D场景图，并在多个数据集上超越了所有学习和随机基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的室内3DSG高层概念生成方法存在两方面的局限：经典方法依赖针对特定概念类别的启发式规则，而学习方法需要为图结构和空间节点特征分别训练独立模型，难以扩展到新类别和更复杂的分层结构。

Method: 提出一个统一的基于自回归扩散的图生成模型，联合学习图结构和空间节点特征。模型从观测到的垂直平面出发，自底向上构建任意阶梯层深度的完整3DSG。此外提出了基于融合Gromov-Wasserstein距离的改编，用于对生成的3DSG进行图级评估。

Result: 该方法在涵盖合成场景、真实建筑平面图和机器人传感器数据的多个3DSG数据集上，一致超越所有基于学习的基线和随机基线；在最大层次结构和真实单层数据上，超越了一类具有目标图大小先知信息的单次模型。

Conclusion: 统一的扩散式生成模型能够有效实现结构-特征联合学习的3DSG生成，并且所提的评估指标为生成场景图的原理性图级比较提供了基础。

Abstract: Indoor 3D Scene Graphs (3DSGs) represent environments as multi-layer hierarchies that connect observed geometric primitives (e.g., planes) to higher-level metric-semantic concepts (e.g., rooms, floors, buildings), enabling incremental spatial reasoning for robotic perception and SLAM. However, classical high-level concept generation approaches rely on hand-crafted rules for specific concept classes, while learning-based methods require separate models for graph structure and spatial node features (e.g., centroids), which limits scalability to novel classes and more complex hierarchies. We propose a unified autoregressive diffusion-based graph generative model that jointly learns structure and features, constructing complete 3DSGs bottom-up from observed vertical planes across arbitrary hierarchy depths. Our method consistently surpasses all learning-based and random baselines across 3DSG datasets spanning synthetic scenes, real architectural floor plans, and robotic sensor data, with varying layout complexity and hierarchy depth, and surpasses a one-shot model with oracle access to the target graph size on the largest hierarchy and on real single-floor data. Finally, we propose an adaptation of the Fused Gromov--Wasserstein distance for principled graph-level evaluation of generated 3DSGs against ground truth.

</details>


### [59] [Adversarial Calibration Attack on Autonomous Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28778)
*Liangkai Liu,Qingzhao Zhang,Kang G. Shin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出对抗标定攻击（ACA），首次针对自动驾驶车辆（AV）的相机-激光雷达在线标定系统发起物理攻击。通过单一的对抗性海报，攻击者可以欺骗失配检测器触发标定过程，并将标定估计器引导至错误的变换，导致系统级错误从感知传播至规划和控制。实验表明，该攻击在KITTI和nuScenes数据集上可造成高达33.9度的平均旋转标定误差，在CARLA仿真中可引发碰撞，并在真实Husky机器人上成功复现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自动驾驶攻击大多假设传感器标定正确，但实际中标定会因振动、温度变化或传感器微小位移而发生漂移，促使在线标定算法在运行时检测和纠正失准。作者识别出在线标定是一个新的攻击平面，因为被篡改的标定更新会持续影响后续融合操作，造成从感知到规划与控制的系统性错误，而这一安全攸关的攻击面此前未被探索。

Method: ACA采用统一的优化算法联合设计对抗海报的几何形状与纹理，实现两个攻击目标：首先欺骗失配检测器触发标定过程，然后操纵标定估计器使其输出错误的变换参数。该方法支持在物理世界中通过打印的对抗海报实施攻击，无需修改车辆软件或硬件。

Result: 在KITTI和nuScenes基准数据集上，ACA诱导高达33.9度的平均旋转标定误差，严重降低目标检测性能；在CARLA仿真器中，攻击在攻击者精心构造的脆弱场景下导致碰撞；在真实Husky机器人平台上，打印的对抗海报成功复现标定错误。

Conclusion: 实验证明在线标定是自动驾驶系统实用且安全攸关的攻击表面。该工作揭示了现有在线标定算法在安全设计上的不足，警示需要在标定系统中引入针对对抗扰动的防御机制，以确保自动驾驶系统在多模态感知融合中的安全性。

Abstract: Autonomous vehicles (AVs) rely on accurate camera-LiDAR calibration for multimodal sensor fusion. In practice, calibration can drift due to vibration, temperature variation, or minor sensor displacement, motivating online calibration algorithms that detect and correct misalignment at runtime while allowing the vehicle to continue operating without a factory visit. Existing AV attacks largely assume correct calibration. We instead identify online sensor calibration as a new attack plane. A corrupted calibration update can persist across subsequent fusion operations, causing system-wide errors that propagate from perception to planning and control. We present Adversarial Calibration Attack (ACA), the first physical attack against camera-LiDAR online calibration. Using a single adversarial poster, ACA first spoofs the miscalibration detector to trigger the calibration process and then steers the calibration estimator toward an incorrect transformation. A unified optimization jointly designs the poster's geometry and texture for both objectives. We evaluate ACA across benchmark datasets, simulation, and physical experiments. On benchmark datasets such as KITTI and nuScenes, ACA induces up to 33.9 degrees mean rotational calibration error, thereby severely degrading object detection. In the CARLA simulator, the attack causes a collision when the corrupted calibration is accepted in vulnerable scenarios crafted by the attacker. On a real Husky robot, a printed adversarial poster successfully reproduces the calibration error. These results demonstrate that online calibration is a practical and safety-critical attack surface for AVs.

</details>


### [60] [Dual Park-Ravani Interpolation of Rigid Motions: Acceleration-Field Continuity and Holonomic Hermite Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28826)
*Daniel Condurache*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文证明Park-Ravani构造（在SO(3)上通过指数化三次正则坐标多项式生成二次连续、坐标系不变的样条）可以不经形式改变地迁移到正交对偶张量群（刚体位移的表示）上。通过对偶扩展指数映射右雅可比及其Fréchet导数，实现了刚体位姿插值和体对偶扭转及其一阶导数的连续性。利用高阶梯刚体运动学，证明所得曲线具有连续的物理加速度场。文章还区分了代数对偶迁移与时间微分延拓，指出两者同时一阶使用需要超对偶代数，并定义了超对偶插值的一阶完整度缺陷，通过三次或五次Hermite插值消除该缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将SO(3)上的Park-Ravani样条构造推广到表示刚体位移的正交对偶张量群，以实现刚体位姿的高阶平滑插值，并确保物理意义上的加速度连续性，而非仅形式上的对偶微分连续性。

Method: 通过对偶扩展指数映射的右Jacobian及其一阶Fréchet导数，将Park-Ravani递推公式迁移到正交对偶张量群；利用超对偶代数区分代数对偶转移与时间微分延拓；通过双对数坐标中的三次或五次Hermite插值消除一阶完整度缺陷。

Result: 实现了刚体位姿的插值和体对偶扭转及其一阶导数的连续性，并在物理层面证明了连续加速度场的存在；通过三姿态非交换实例验证了递推公式、所有结点连续性以及从米到毫米的维度协变性；给出了超对偶插值一阶完整度缺陷的精确反例并成功消除。

Conclusion: Park-Ravani构造可自然推广到刚体运动学领域，且通过双对数坐标的Hermite插值能修复超对偶插值中的完整度缺陷，为刚体位姿的高阶平滑插值提供了可靠的理论框架。

Abstract: The Park-Ravani construction generates a twice continuously differentiable, frame-invariant spline on SO(3) by exponentiating cubic canonical-coordinate polynomials. We show that the construction transfers, without changing form, to the group of orthogonal dual tensors, a representation of rigid displacements. The transferred recurrence is stated compactly through the dual extension of the right Jacobian of the exponential map and its first Fréchet derivative. This yields interpolation of prescribed rigid poses and continuity of the body dual twist and its first derivative. Using the higher-order rigid-body kinematics of dual spatial twists, we then prove that the resulting curve has a continuous physical acceleration field, not merely a continuous quantity obtained by formally differentiating the dual part of a twist. We also distinguish algebraic dual transfer from temporal differential prolongation: their simultaneous first-order use takes place in a hyper-dual algebra, and interpolation of arbitrary prolonged nodal data need not be holonomic. A noncommuting three-pose example verifies the recurrence, all knot continuity statements, and dimensional covariance under a change from meters to millimeters. We define and analyze the first-order holonomy defect of a generic hyper-dual interpolant, exhibit an exact counterexample, and remove the defect by cubic or quintic Hermite interpolation in dual logarithmic coordinates.

</details>


### [61] [Brain-Language-Action (BLA) Models: Language-Conditioned EEG for Robotics Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28967)
*Alexandr Plashchinsky*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出脑-语言-动作（BLA）模型框架，通过语言调节神经表征的解析方式，将少量可区分的脑状态动态映射到更大的机器人动作空间，在脑电无人机控制任务中实现了90%的逐token准确率，证明语言条件可以扩展脑机接口的有效控制范围而无需增加可直接区分的神经状态数量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于脑电的机器人控制被建模为直接分类问题，将神经信号映射到固定离散动作集。但由于脑电信号可分性有限且噪声高，这种方法难以扩展到细粒度的机器人控制空间。需要一种新框架来突破这一规模限制。

Method: BLA模型采用两阶段训练：首先评估多种EEG编码器架构，将250Hz、3.5秒、22通道的运动想象脑电样本转换为五个128维脑token嵌入；然后将这些嵌入投影到预训练大语言模型的嵌入空间中，与语言指令联合微调，自回归生成结构化的三token无人机动作。

Result: 在BCI Competition IV 2a数据集上，在840种可能的语言定义映射（4种神经状态对应7种飞行动作组合）中，BLA模型在评估时实现了90%的逐token准确率，验证了语言条件化能够有效扩展脑电机器人接口的控制范围。

Conclusion: 语言条件化可以在不增加可直接区分神经状态数量的情况下扩大脑电机器人接口的有效控制范围，为精细机器人控制与脑机接口的结合提供了新的思路和初步证据。

Abstract: Electroencephalography (EEG)-based robotic control is commonly formulated as a direct classification problem, in which electrical neural signals are mapped to a fixed set of discrete actions. However, the limited separability and high noise of EEG signals make it difficult to scale this approach to fine-grained robotic control spaces. We introduce Brain-Language-Action (BLA) models, a framework in which language conditions the interpretation of neural representations for robotic action generation. In a BLA, a small set of reliably distinguishable brain states can be dynamically associated with different actions through a language-defined control mapping, allowing a small number of neural classes to apply to a larger global action space.
  We develop a proof-of-concept BLA for drone control using motor-imagery EEG from the BCI Competition IV 2a dataset. The system is trained in two stages. First, we evaluate multiple candidate EEG encoder architectures using subject-specific four-class motor-imagery classification, converting 250Hz, 3.5-second, 22-channel EEG samples into five 128-dimensional brain-token embeddings. Second, these embeddings are projected into the embedding space of a pretrained large language model (LLM) and jointly fine-tuned with language instructions to autoregressively generate structured three-token drone actions. Across 840 possible language-defined mappings between four neural states and seven flight action combinations, the resulting BLA achieves 90% per-token accuracy during evaluation. These results provide an initial demonstration that language conditioning can expand the effective control range of EEG-based robotic interfaces without requiring a corresponding increase in the number of directly distinguishable neural states.

</details>


### [62] [Hydra: A Navigation World Action Model with Discrete Latent Planning and Continuous Flow-Matching Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28995)
*Mohammad Nazeri,Alexandyr Card,Samira Huber,Anuj Pokhrel,Yujun Wang,Ruben Hammele,Daeun Song,Sören Pirk,Xuesu Xiao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Hydra是一种离散世界动作模型，通过将规划器嵌入生成模型内部，在统一潜空间中完成采样与评估，避免解码回像素空间，实现实时控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型让机器人能想象未来，但生成模型与规划器在解耦的流形上运作，导致规划器每次评估候选方案都需解码回高维像素空间，成为实时控制的瓶颈。

Method: Hydra构建覆盖视觉状态、物理位姿和控制动作的统一潜流形，通过模态特定的VQ瓶颈压缩为离散的动力学意图和视觉状态词汇表；规划器直接在共享流形中采样，并以Kinematic-Perceptual Cost在离散空间中评估候选方案（即离散潜在规划DLP）；同时结合条件Flow Matching将所选意图映射为连续控制轨迹。

Result: 在两个物理机器人平台上，Hydra在目标导向规划任务上优于最先进的世界模型，在闭环执行能力上达到或超越领先的反应式基础策略。

Conclusion: Hydra通过将规划器嵌入模型内部并实现离散潜在规划，消除了解码瓶颈，同时结合Flow Matching保证连续控制能力，为世界模型驱动的实时机器人控制提供了有效方案。

Abstract: World models let robots imagine possible futures, but exploiting this capability for real-time control is bottlenecked by a representation misalignment: the generative model and the planner operate on decoupled manifolds, so the planner has no shared structure to search over and must instead decode every candidate back into high-dimensional pixel space to evaluate it. This decoding step is a major obstacle to real-time control on physical hardware. In this paper, we present Hydra, a discrete World Action Model that closes this gap by moving the planner, both the sampler and the evaluator, inside the model. Hydra establishes a unified latent manifold over visual states, physical poses, and control actions, then compresses this manifold through modality-specific Vector-Quantized bottlenecks into discrete vocabularies of kinodynamic intents and visual states. Because candidates are now drawn directly from this shared manifold, sampling is informed by the model's own understanding of the observation rather than proposed blind, and evaluation happens natively within the discrete space: candidates are ranked by a Kinematic-Perceptual Cost, without ever decoding to pixels. We term this Discrete Latent Planning (DLP). Because planning over discrete intents alone cannot supply the smooth, continuous commands physical actuation requires, Hydra pairs DLP with conditional Flow Matching, which maps each selected intent to a continuous trajectory for execution. Evaluated on two physical robotic platforms, Hydra outperforms state-of-the-art world models in goal-directed planning, while matching or exceeding the closed-loop execution capabilities of leading reactive foundation policies.

</details>


### [63] [Coding What Matters: A Semantic-Aware Memory Interface for Energy-Efficient Perception in Autonomous Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29000)
*Haohua Que,Handong Yao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MotiMem-Omega是一种语义感知的内存接口编码器，通过降低图像写入内存时的位1密度和翻转活动来减少自动驾驶系统传感器到内存路径的能耗，同时保持感知预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶车辆在感知运行前将高分辨率环视相机帧流式写入内存，该路径的能耗由位1密度和切换活动决定而非像素语义。现有方法未能在降低此类能耗的同时保留感知精度，尤其对于弱势道路使用者等关键交通参与者。

Method: 提出MotiMem-Omega语义感知编码器：利用语义重要性场保护交通参与者（特别是VRU），对天空和背景分配较低保真度；通过跨数据集位敏感性扫描确定类别权重并设置安全下限；每个图像块通过最小化联合能量-失真代价选择精度层级；在可用姿态信息时采用运动补偿先验来跨帧携带受保护区域。

Result: 在29个检测器（覆盖12个驾驶数据集）、5个占据模型和5个分割网络上测试：保留约90%检测mAP、91%弱势道路使用者召回率、超过98%占据精度，分割精度保持最佳。前相机位1密度降低52%，建模内存接口能耗降低约36%（文献系数扫描下限为27%）。在相同或更低位1密度下，保留率优于基线和能量匹配截断方法；图像编解码器虽保持精度但不减少内存接口能耗。

Conclusion: MotiMem-Omega证明语义感知的编码策略可以在显著降低自动驾驶内存接口能耗的同时有效保留感知性能，为传感器到内存端到端路径的能效优化提供了新思路。

Abstract: Autonomous vehicles stream high-resolution surround-camera frames into memory before perception runs. This sensor-to-memory path consumes energy when cells store ones and adjacent bytes toggle on the data bus, so its cost follows bit-1 density and switching activity rather than pixel semantics. We present MotiMem-Omega, a semantic-aware memory-interface coder that lowers this cost while preserving perception predictions. Its semantic importance field protects traffic participants, especially vulnerable road users, while assigning lower fidelity to sky and empty background. Cross-dataset bit-sensitivity sweeps determine class weights, with a safety floor for pedestrians, cyclists, and motorcyclists. Each image block then selects a precision tier by minimizing a joint energy-distortion cost. When ego pose is available, a motion-compensated prior carries protected regions between frames. We estimate interface-energy reduction from the two measured proxies using a coefficient-swept memory-energy model. Across 29 detectors on 12 driving datasets, 5 occupancy models, and 5 segmentation networks, MotiMem-Omega retains about 90% of detection mean average precision, 91% of vulnerable-road-user recall, over 98% of occupancy accuracy, and the strongest segmentation retention among energy-reducing methods. It reduces front-camera bit-1 density by 52%, corresponding to a modeled memory-interface energy reduction near 36%, with a lower end of 27% under the literature coefficient sweep. It also gives higher retention than the baseline and energy-matched truncation at the same or lower bit-1 density, whereas image codecs preserve accuracy without reducing memory-interface energy.

</details>


### [64] [A Degradation-Tolerance Benchmark for Camera-Only End-to-End Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29005)
*Haohua Que,Handong Yao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出DriveDegrade基准，用于评估相机端到端驾驶模型对图像退化的鲁棒性。研究发现：轻度退化影响不大但存在清晰阈值；不同退化类型影响差异大（模糊、JPEG、雨滴破坏最大）；通过区分图像退化与信息删除两个轴，可以量化规划器的鲁棒性而不误判为稳健。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的退化鲁棒性基准主要针对检测或鸟瞰图感知，而非端到端驾驶的规划输出。随着相机端到端驾驶模型接近部署，图像退化（模糊、噪声、天气等）对规划决策的影响尚不清楚，缺乏针对规划输出的退化容忍度评估工具。

Method: 构建DriveDegrade基准：将16种退化类型、5种严重程度在线注入图像加载器，应用于15个策略，分别评估nuScenes和NAVSIM上的开环规划以及CARLA闭环场景。并提出双轴分析框架：区分图像退化（质量损失）与信息删除，以识别规划器是否依赖捷径（如自我状态）而非真正读取相机信息。

Result: 轻度退化几乎不影响规划，但导致故障的退化类型存在中等严重程度的清晰阈值。鲁棒性与退化类型相关：模糊、JPEG和雨滴损伤最严重，而天气和比特错误在更大范围内仍可容忍。发布的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）规划器在两个轴上均表现平坦，蒙住全部六个摄像头仅损失11.5%的性能，表明其依赖捷径而非视觉信息。

Conclusion: DriveDegrade为相机端到端驾驶的退化鲁棒性提供了系统评估方法，并通过双轴分析揭示了一些看似稳健的规划器实际并未真正使用视觉信息，而是依赖捷径。该基准有助于在部署前识别模型的真实鲁棒性和潜在缺陷。

Abstract: Camera-only end-to-end (E2E) driving models are nearing deployment, where the camera stream is degraded by blur, noise, low light, weather, frame loss, and memory faults. How much a policy tolerates before its driving breaks is unclear. Corruption-robustness benchmarks target detection or bird's-eye-view perception, not the planning output that drives the car. We present DriveDegrade, a benchmark for image-degradation tolerance in camera-only E2E driving. Sixteen corruption families at five severities are injected on the fly inside the image loader, one operator reaching fifteen policies, and we evaluate open-loop planning on nuScenes and NAVSIM plus a CARLA closed-loop anchor. First, mild degradation barely affects planning, and the families that break it have a clear threshold at mid severity. Second, fragility is corruption-dependent: blur, JPEG, and raindrop damage planning most, while weather and bit error are tolerated far into the range. Third, a flat curve is ambiguous, so we separate corruptions that degrade the image from those that remove it. A planner that reads its camera must lose accuracy when information is deleted, whatever it does under quality loss. On these two axes the planners separate sharply, quantifying the ego-status shortcut without mistaking indifference for robustness. A released vision-language-action planner is flat on both axes, and blinding all six of its cameras costs it only 11.5 percent.

</details>


### [65] [Teaching Robot Policies to Humans Using Erroneous Examples](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29023)
*Rithika Narayan,Suresh Kumaar Jayaraman,Henny Admoni*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出将传统课堂中"错误示例"教学方法应用于机器人策略教学，通过让参与者观看错误示范并纠正动作来学习机器人策略。研究发现，观看错误示范并口头推理预测机器人动作能够提高策略记忆保持率，且使用逆向强化学习式推理的参与者表现最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人机协作中，让机器人策略对人类透明至关重要。虽然已有基于示范的教学方法，但没有一种方法被证明在所有领域、难度和学习者中都有效，如何最有效地向人类传授机器人策略仍是一个开放问题。

Method: 将传统课堂中的错误示例教学法扩展应用于机器人策略教学框架。进行用户研究，让参与者观看不正确演示并纠正动作以符合实际策略，同时将参与者分类为不同的学习风格进行分析。

Result: 观看错误示范并口头推理预测机器人动作能提高策略随时间保持的效果，与传统课堂中错误示例的效果一致。采用逆向强化学习式推理的参与者在策略预测任务中表现最佳。

Conclusion: 错误示范教学法可有效用于机器人策略教学，推进了机器人向人类传授其策略的方法研究。

Abstract: Human-robot collaboration describes the process of humans and autonomous agents working together to accomplish common goals. This process is facilitated best when robot policies, or behaviors in different situations, are made transparent to humans. Demonstration-based explanations have been a focus of human-robot collaboration research, and the field has frequently drawn upon literature from education to improve how humans are taught robot policies. However, no single teaching method has been proven effective across domains, difficulties, learners, and other variables; the question of how humans can most effectively be taught robot policies remains open.
  In traditional classrooms, learners are shown erroneous examples, in which they reflect on and correct incorrect responses to understand common pitfalls when learning a concept. We propose using erroneous examples to teach robot policies, extending an existing policy teaching framework. We conduct a user study in which participants view incorrect demonstrations of robot behavior and correct the actions to align with the actual policy. Our findings suggest that viewing these incorrect demonstrations and verbalizing one's reasoning in predicting a robot's actions improves retention of the policy over time, in agreement with the effect of erroneous examples in classrooms. We also categorize participants into distinct learning styles and establish that participants using inverse reinforcement learning-like reasoning perform best on policy prediction tasks. With this work, we aim to advance the methods by which robots educate humans on their policies.

</details>


### [66] [DREAM: Deployment-Time Demonstration Generation via Real-to-Sim for Scalable Policy Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29078)
*Makoto Sato,Tatsuya Matsushima,Yutaka Matsuo,Yusuke Iwasawa*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出DREAM框架，无需人工遥操作演示，即可为预训练VLA模型在目标工作空间自动生成微调数据。通过重建工作空间、LLM翻译指令为符号任务目标、任务与运动规划生成可行轨迹、数据增强与验证，最终渲染为图像-动作样本用于VLA微调，降低了新工作空间适配的数据采集成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型在语言条件机器人操作中取得进展，但在新工作空间中提升性能仍需该环境下的动作标注数据。人工遥操作采集数据成本高昂，尤其当每个工作空间、物体布局或任务都需要新的演示时，需要一种无需任务特定人工演示的数据生成方法。

Method: DREAM框架包含四个步骤：1) 重建工作空间；2) 利用大语言模型将语言指令自动翻译为符号任务目标和成功标准；3) 使用任务与运动规划生成可行的机器人轨迹；4) 增强轨迹（随机化物体配置）、通过生成的成功标准验证，并渲染为图像-动作样本用于VLA微调。

Result: 通过真实机器人语言条件操作任务实验，研究DREAM能否作为可扩展的数据采集系统，评估在部署工作空间上微调后是否优于直接部署，以及相比人工遥操作在新工作空间适配VLA时的数据采集成本对比。

Conclusion: DREAM提供了一种无需人工演示的可扩展VLA微调数据生成方案，有望在降低数据采集成本的同时提升VLA在新工作空间的适应性能。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models have made strong progress in language-conditioned robot manipulation, but improving their performance in a new workspace still often requires action-labeled data from that environment. Collecting such data by human teleoperation is costly, especially when each workspace, object arrangement, or task may require new demonstrations. We present DREAM, a framework that generates fine-tuning data for a pretrained VLA from a captured workspace and a language instruction, without requiring a task-specific human demonstration. DREAM reconstructs the workspace, automatically translates the instruction into symbolic task goals and success criteria using a large language model, and uses task-and-motion planning to generate feasible robot trajectories. The planned trajectories are augmented across randomized object configurations, verified by the generated success criteria, and rendered into image-action examples for VLA fine-tuning. Through real-robot experiments on language-conditioned manipulation tasks, we study whether DREAM can serve as a scalable data-collection system for the deployment workspace by examining whether fine-tuning on its automatically generated data improves success over direct deployment and how its data-collection cost compares with human teleoperation when adapting a VLA to a new workspace.

</details>


### [67] [GHOST in the Robots: Real-Time Exocentric Dual-Robot VR Teleoperation from Onboard Cameras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29080)
*Yichen Wei,Faisal Zaghloul,Soujanya C Aryal,Aanya K. Agrawal,Chengfan Li,Jason Xinyu Liu,James Tompkin,Stefanie Tellex*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GHOST是一个开源VR遥操作系统，允许单个操作员通过低级直接命令同时控制两台移动机械臂，利用学习式点云补全提升空间感知，实验表明相比平板界面成功率和速度均有显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多机器人遥操作存在根本性挑战：需要连贯地呈现传感器数据，并让操作员在低延迟下管理多个机器人底座、手臂和摄像头。现存系统要么需要多名操作员，要么依赖自主性或只能进行高级命令，缺乏支持单人低级直接控制的解决方案。

Method: GHOST通过对齐机器人RGB-D相机的实时点云创建外心3D工作空间，利用学习式补全改善场景覆盖以增强操作员空间感知；控制方面采用模式切换架构，允许操作员单独或同时指挥两个机器人。

Result: 15名新手参与者的实验表明，GHOST的成功率是现成平板界面的1.6-4倍；在专家参与的九项双机器人挑战任务中，系统完成了两项平板界面无法实现的任务，平均速度快1.47倍。

Conclusion: GHOST验证了通过VR进行单人多移动机械臂低级直接遥操作的可行性，在成功率和效率上均优于传统平板界面，代码和网站已开源供社区使用。

Abstract: Teleoperating multiple robots simultaneously enables additional views and coordinated control. Yet, it poses fundamental challenges: the system must present sensor data cohesively and allow operators to manage multiple robot bases, arms, and cameras while maintaining low latency. Current multi-robot teleoperation systems require multiple operators, rely on autonomy, or restrict operators to high-level commands. We present GHOST: an open-source VR teleoperation system that enables single operator control of two mobile manipulators via direct lowlevel commands using only onboard sensing. GHOST creates an exocentric 3D workspace by aligning real-time point clouds from the robots' RGB-D cameras, where scene coverage is improved through learning-based completion to aid operator spatial awareness. For control, the operator uses a mode-switching architecture to command either robot individually or both robots simultaneously. Experiments with 15 novice participants demonstrate 1.6-4x the success rate of an off-the-shelf tablet interface. For experts across nine challenging dual-robot tasks, our system enabled completion of two tasks that were infeasible with the tablet, and was 1.47x faster on average than the tablet. Website and code: https://h2r.github.io/GHOST/.

</details>


### [68] [Agri-Sim: Agricultural Simulation Platform for Embodied Intelligence Evaluation in Greenhouse Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29100)
*Shuhan Shi,Zhenfeng Xue,Minghao Mei,Chao Zheng,Nan Li,Zhonghua Miao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Agri-Sim，一个基于Unity和ROS2的农业机器人仿真平台，支持温室场景构建、虚拟感知、自主导航、运动规划和操控任务执行。实验验证了温室导航和双臂番茄采摘的完整闭环仿真流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 农业机器人开发需要能够同时支持真实场景构建、虚拟感知、自主导航、运动规划和操控任务执行的仿真环境，以加速算法开发和验证。

Method: 构建基于Unity和ROS2的仿真平台，Unity负责场景渲染、刚体动力学、碰撞检测、虚拟传感和任务状态执行，ROS2与MoveIt 2提供定位、导航、碰撞感知运动规划、逆运动学和轨迹生成，通过双向通信接口集成。

Result: 实验覆盖虚拟传感器发布、ROS2导航、碰撞感知运动规划、移动基座控制、番茄获取、双臂交接和箱体放置，验证了平台支持闭环集成和可重复的功能评估。

Conclusion: Agri-Sim为农业机器人导航和操控工作流提供了可控温室环境下的闭环集成和可重复功能评估基础，为后续算法开发与Sim-to-Real研究提供了实用平台。

Abstract: Agricultural-robot development requires simulation environments that can jointly support realistic scene construction, virtual sensing, autonomous navigation, motion planning, and manipulation-task execution. This paper presents Agri-Sim, a Unity and ROS2-based simulation platform for the closed-loop development and functional evaluation of agricultural robots. The platform contains a configurable tomato-greenhouse environment, a mobile dual-arm harvesting robot, virtual RGB-D, LiDAR, IMU, and joint sensors, and a bidirectional communication interface between Unity and ROS2. Unity is responsible for scene rendering, rigid-body dynamics, collision detection, virtual sensing, and task-state execution, whereas ROS2 and MoveIt 2 provide localization, navigation, collision-aware motion planning, inverse kinematics, and trajectory generation. Autonomous greenhouse navigation and dual-arm tomato harvesting were used to evaluate the complete simulation workflow. The experiments covered virtual sensor publication, ROS2-based navigation, collision-aware motion planning, mobile-base control, tomato acquisition, inter-arm handover, and box placement.
